Return True if modified.\"\"\"\n    if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in p_text(p, ns):\n        return False\n    modified = False\n    for t in p.findall('.//hp:t', ns):\n        if t.text is None:\n            continue\n        old = t.text\n        new = old\n        for a,b in replacements:\n            if a and a in new:\n                new = new.replace(a,b)\n        if new != old:\n            t.text = new\n            modified = True\n    return modified\n\n\ndef cleanup_customer_paragraph(p, ns, customer_name):\n    \"\"\"Special-case: customer is split across runs; set to exactly '고객사: {customer_name}'.\"\"\"\n    if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in p_text(p, ns):\n        return False\n    txt = p_text(p, ns)\n    if not txt.startswith('고객사:'):\n        return False\n    t_nodes = p.findall('.//hp:t', ns)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False\n    #
